# Archived evidence originals

Generated by `python scripts/evidence-resolver.py index`; do not edit manually.

These are preserved original identities, not public download grants or new performance samples. Sensitive or unreviewed originals remain in private R2. [Retrieval and verification](evidence-storage.md#archived-original-alias-resolution) uses the [versioned registry](evidence-registry.v1.json). Unknown historical replay/provenance remains unknown; current source never substitutes for missing bytes.
